Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on innate structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 673. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 477 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the innate space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 673 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 477. QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The innate operator L associated with invariance principle theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 41. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 567 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
